# Documentation index

Generated from Git tracked and nonignored working files in `docs/`. Run `node <skill>/scripts/docs-index.mjs --root <repo> --docs-dir <relative-dir>` after changing document paths or titles.
This lists files, not current authority or acceptance status. Read each document and its linked evidence before relying on a claim.
For first and last recorded Git commit dates, run `node <skill>/scripts/docs-index.mjs --dates --root <repo> --docs-dir <relative-dir>`; see `build/docs-index-dates.tsv`. Dates do not establish original creation or uncommitted edit time.
